\\brief Allocate new buffer.\n *\n * \\return pointer to buffer on success.\n * \\return NULL on failure.\n */\nJA3Buffer *Ja3BufferInit(void)\n{\n    JA3Buffer *buffer = SCCalloc(1, sizeof(JA3Buffer));\n    if (buffer == NULL) {\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    return buffer;\n}\n\n/**\n * \\brief Free allocated buffer.\n *\n * \\param buffer The buffer to free.\n */\nvoid Ja3BufferFree(JA3Buffer **buffer)\n{\n    DEBUG_VALIDATE_BUG_ON(*buffer == NULL);\n\n    if ((*buffer)->data != NULL) {\n        SCFree((*buffer)->data);\n        (*buffer)->data = NULL;\n    }\n\n    SCFree(*buffer);\n    *buffer = NULL;\n}\n\n#ifdef HAVE_JA3\n\n/**\n * \\internal\n * \\brief Resize buffer if it is full.\n *\n * \\param buffer The buffer.\n * \\param len    The length of the data that should fit into the buffer.\n *\n * \\retval 0 on success.\n * \\retval -1 on failure.\n */\nstatic int Ja3BufferResizeIfFull(JA3Buffer *buffer, uint32_t len)\n{\n    DEBUG_VALIDATE_BUG_ON(buffer == NULL);\n\n    while (buffer->used + len + 2 > buffer->size)\n    {\n        buffer->size *= 2;\n        char *tmp = SCRealloc(buffer->data, buffer->size);\n        if (tmp == NULL) {\n            SCLogError(\"Error resizing JA3 buffer\");\n            return -1;\n        }\n        buffer->data = tmp;\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * \\brief Append buffer to buffer.\n *\n * Append the second buffer to the first and then free it.\n *\n * \\param buffer1 The first buffer.\n * \\param buffer2 The second buffer.\n *\n * \\retval 0 on success.\n * \\retval -1 on failure.\n */\nint Ja3BufferAppendBuffer(JA3Buffer **buffer1, JA3Buffer **buffer2)\n{\n    if (*buffer1 == NULL || *buffer2 == NULL) {\n        SCLogError(\"Buffers should not be NULL\");\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* If buffer1 contains no data, then we just copy the second buffer\n       instead of appending its data. */\n    if ((*buffer1)->data == NULL) {\n        (*buffer1)->data = (*buffer2)->data;\n        (*buffer1)->used = (*buffer2)->used;\n        (*buffer1)->size = (*buffer2)->size;\n        SCFree(*buffer2);\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    int rc = Ja3BufferResizeIfFull(*buffer1, (*buffer2)->used);\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        Ja3BufferFree(buffer1);\n        Ja3BufferFree(buffer2);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    if ((*buffer2)->used == 0) {\n        (*buffer1)->used += snprintf((*buffer1)->data + (*buffer1)->used,\n                                     (*buffer1)->size - (*buffer1)->used, \",\");\n    } else {\n        (*buffer1)->used += snprintf((*buffer1)->data + (*buffer1)->used,\n                                     (*buffer1)->size - (*buffer1)->used, \",%s\",\n                                     (*buffer2)->data);\n    }\n\n    Ja3BufferFree(buffer2);\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * \\internal\n * \\brief Return number of digits in number.\n *\n * \\param num The number.\n *\n * \\return digits Number of digits.\n */\nstatic uint32_t NumberOfDigits(uint32_t num)\n{\n    if (num < 10) {\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    return 1 + NumberOfDigits(num / 10);\n}\n\n/**\n * \\brief Add value to buffer.\n *\n * \\param buffer The buffer.\n * \\param value  The value.\n *\n * \\retval 0 on success.\n * \\retval -1 on failure.\n */\nint Ja3BufferAddValue(JA3Buffer **buffer, uint32_t value)\n{\n    if (*buffer == NULL) {\n        SCLogError(\"Buffer should not be NULL\");\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    if ((*buffer)->data == NULL) {\n        (*buffer)->data = SCMalloc(JA3_BUFFER_INITIAL_SIZE);\n        if ((*buffer)->data == NULL) {\n            SCLogError(\"Error allocating memory for JA3 data\");\n            Ja3BufferFree(buffer);\n            return -1;\n        }\n        (*buffer)->size = JA3_BUFFER_INITIAL_SIZE;\n    }\n\n    uint32_t value_len = NumberOfDigits(value);\n\n    int rc = Ja3BufferResizeIfFull(*buffer, value_len);\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        Ja3BufferFree(buffer);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    if ((*buffer)->used == 0) {\n        (*buffer)->used += snprintf((*buffer)->data, (*buffer)->size, \"%u\", value);\n    }\n    else {\n        (*buffer)->used += snprintf(\n                (*buffer)->data + (*buffer)->used, (*buffer)->size - (*buffer)->used, \"-%u\", value);\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * \\brief Generate Ja3 hash string.\n *\n * \\param buffer The Ja3 buffer.\n *\n * \\retval pointer to hash string on success.\n * \\retval NULL on failure.\n */\nchar *Ja3GenerateHash(JA3Buffer *buffer)\n{\n    if (buffer == NULL) {\n        SCLogError(\"Buffer should not be NULL\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    if (buffer->data == NULL) {\n        SCLogError(\"Buffer data should not be NULL\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    char *ja3_hash = SCMalloc(SC_MD5_HEX_LEN + 1);\n    if (ja3_hash == NULL) {\n        SCLogError(\"Error allocating memory for JA3 hash\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    SCMd5HashBufferToHex((unsigned char *)buffer->data, buffer->used, ja3_hash, SC_MD5_HEX_LEN + 1);\n    return ja3_hash;\n}\n\n/**\n * \\brief Check if JA3 is disabled.\n *\n * Issue warning if JA3 is disabled or if we are lacking support for JA3.\n *\n * \\param type Type to add to warning.\n *\n * \\retval 1 if disabled.\n * \\retval 0 otherwise.\n */\nint Ja3IsDisabled(const char *type)\n{\n    bool is_enabled = SSLJA3IsEnabled();\n    if (is_enabled == 0) {\n        if (strcmp(type, \"rule\") != 0) {\n            SCLogWarning(\"JA3 is disabled, skipping %s\", type);\n        }\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\nInspectionBuffer *Ja3DetectGetHash(DetectEngineThreadCtx *det_ctx,\n        const DetectEngineTransforms *transforms, Flow *_f, const uint8_t _flow_flags, void *txv,\n        const int list_id)\n{\n    InspectionBuffer *buffer = InspectionBufferGet(det_ctx, list_id);\n    if (buffer->inspect == NULL) {\n        uint32_t b_len = 0;\n        const uint8_t *b = NULL;\n\n        if (!SCQuicTxGetJa3(txv, STREAM_TOSERVER | STREAM_TOCLIENT, &b, &b_len))\n            return NULL;\n        if (b == NULL || b_len == 0)\n            return NULL;\n\n        uint8_t ja3_hash[SC_MD5_HEX_LEN + 1];\n        // this adds a final zero\n        SCMd5HashBufferToHex(b, b_len, (char *)ja3_hash, SC_MD5_HEX_LEN + 1);\n\n        InspectionBufferSetup(det_ctx, list_id, buffer, NULL, 0);\n        InspectionBufferCopy(buffer, ja3_hash, SC_MD5_HEX_LEN);\n        InspectionBufferApplyTransforms(det_ctx, buffer, transforms);\n    }\n    return buffer;\n}\n\nInspectionBuffer *Ja3DetectGetString(DetectEngineThreadCtx *det_ctx,\n        const DetectEngineTransforms *transforms, Flow *_f, const uint8_t _flow_flags, void *txv,\n        const int list_id)\n{\n    InspectionBuffer *buffer = InspectionBufferGet(det_ctx, list_id);\n    if (buffer->inspect == NULL) {\n        uint32_t b_len = 0;\n        const uint8_t *b = NULL;\n\n        if {\n55\t            Some(TlsVersion::Tls13) => \"13\",\n56\t            Some(TlsVersion::Tls12) => \"12\",\n57\t            Some(TlsVersion::Tls11) => \"11\",\n58\t            Some(TlsVersion::Tls10) => \"10\",\n59\t            Some(TlsVersion::Ssl30) => \"s3\",\n60\t            // the TLS parser does not support SSL 1.0 and 2.0 hence no\n61\t            // support for \"s1\"/\"s2\"\n62\t            _ => \"00\",\n63\t        }\n64\t    }\n65\t\n66\t    fn format_alpn(alpn: Option<&Vec<u8>>) -> [char; 2] {\n67\t        let mut ret = ['0', '0'];\n68\t\n69\t        if let Some(alpn) = alpn {\n70\t            if !alpn.is_empty() {\n71\t                // If the first ALPN value is only a single character, then that character is treated as both the first and last character.\n72\t                if alpn.len() == 2 {\n73\t                    // GREASE values are 2 bytes, so this could be one -- check\n74\t                    let v: u16 = ((alpn[0] as u16) << 8) | alpn[alpn.len() - 1] as u16;\n75\t                    if HandshakeParams::is_grease(v) {\n76\t                        return ret;\n77\t                    }\n78\t                }\n79\t                if !alpn[0].is_ascii_alphanumeric() || !alpn[alpn.len() - 1].is_ascii_alphanumeric()\n80\t                {\n81\t                    // If the first or last byte of the first ALPN is non-alphanumeric (meaning not 0x30-0x39, 0x41-0x5A, or 0x61-0x7A), then we print the first and last characters of the hex representation of the first ALPN instead.\n82\t                    ret[0] = char::from(HEX[(alpn[0] >> 4) as usize]);\n83\t                    ret[1] = char::from(HEX[(alpn[alpn.len() - 1] & 0xF) as usize]);\n84\t                } else {\n85\t                    ret[0] = char::from(alpn[0]);\n86\t                    ret[1] = char::from(alpn[alpn.len() - 1]);\n87\t                }\n88\t            }\n89\t        }\n90\t        ret\n91\t    }\n92\t}\n93\t\n94\t#[cfg(feature = \"ja4\")]\n95\timpl JA4Impl for JA4 {\n96\t    fn try_new(hs: &HandshakeParams) -> Option<Self> {\n97\t        // All non-GREASE extensions are stored to produce a more verbose, complete output\n98\t        // of extensions but we need to omit ALPN & SNI extensions from the JA4_a hash.\n99\t        let mut exts = hs\n100\t            .extensions\n101\t            .iter()\n102\t            .filter(|&ext| {\n103\t                *ext != TlsExtensionType::ApplicationLayerProtocolNegotiation\n104\t                    && *ext != TlsExtensionType::ServerName\n105\t            })\n106\t            .collect::<Vec<&TlsExtensionType>>();\n107\t\n108\t        let alpn = Self::format_alpn(hs.alpns.first());\n109\t\n110\t        // Calculate JA4_a\n111\t        let ja4_a = format!(\n112\t            \"{proto}{version}{sni}{nof_c:02}{nof_e:02}{al1}{al2}\",\n113\t            proto = if hs.quic { \"q\" } else { \"t\" },\n114\t            version = Self::version_to_ja4code(hs.tls_version),\n115\t            sni = if hs.domain { \"d\" } else { \"i\" },\n116\t            nof_c = min(99, hs.ciphersuites.len()),\n117\t            nof_e = min(99, hs.extensions.len()),\n118\t            al1 = alpn[0],\n119\t            al2 = alpn[1]\n120\t        );\n121\t\n122\t        // Calculate JA4_b\n123\t        let mut sorted_ciphers = hs.ciphersuites.to_vec();\n124\t        sorted_ciphers.sort_by(|a, b| u16::from(*a).cmp(&u16::from(*b)));\n125\t        let sorted_cipherstrings: Vec<String> = sorted_ciphers\n126\t            .iter()\n127\t            .map(|v| format!(\"{:04x}\", u16::from(*v)))\n128\t            .collect();\n129\t        let mut sha = Sha256::new();\n130\t        let ja4_b_raw = sorted_cipherstrings.join(\",\");\n131\t        sha.update(&ja4_b_raw);\n132\t        let mut ja4_b = format!(\"{:x}\", sha.finalize_reset());\n133\t        ja4_b.truncate(12);\n134\t\n135\t        // Calculate JA4_c\n136\t        exts.sort_by(|&a, &b| u16::from(*a).cmp(&u16::from(*b)));\n137\t        let sorted_extstrings: Vec<String> = exts\n138\t            .into_iter()\n139\t            .map(|&v| format!(\"{:04x}\", u16::from(v)))\n140\t            .collect();\n141\t        let ja4_c1_raw = sorted_extstrings.join(\",\");\n142\t        let unsorted_sigalgostrings: Vec<String> = hs\n143\t            .signature_algorithms\n144\t            .iter()\n145\t            .map(|v| format!(\"{:04x}\", (*v)))\n146\t            .collect();\n147\t        let ja4_c2_raw = unsorted_sigalgostrings.join(\",\");\n148\t        let ja4_c_raw = format!(\"{}_{}\", ja4_c1_raw, ja4_c2_raw);\n149\t        sha.update(&ja4_c_raw);\n150\t        let mut ja4_c = format!(\"{:x}\", sha.finalize());\n151\t        ja4_c.truncate(12);\n152\t\n153\t        Some(Self {\n154\t            hash: format!(\"{}_{}_{}\", ja4_a, ja4_b, ja4_c),\n155\t        })\n156\t    }\n157\t}\n158\t\n159\t#[cfg(not(feature = \"ja4\"))]\n160\timpl JA4Impl for JA4 {\n161\t    fn try_new(_hs: &HandshakeParams) -> Option<Self> {\n162\t        None\n163\t    }\n164\t}\n165\t\n166\t// crate::jsonbuilder::HEX;\n#[cfg(feature = \"ja4\")]\nuse digest::Digest;\n#[cfg(feature = \"ja4\")]\nuse sha2::Sha256;\n#[cfg(feature = \"ja4\")]\nuse std::cmp::min;\n#[cfg(feature = \"ja4\")]\nuse tls_parser::{TlsExtensionType, TlsVersion};\n\nuse crate::handshake::HandshakeParams;\n\npub const JA4_HEX_LEN: usize = 36;\n\npub(crate) trait JA4Impl {\n    fn try_new(hs: &HandshakeParams) -> Option<JA4>;\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]\npub struct JA4 {\n    hash: String,\n}\n\nimpl AsRef<str> for JA4 {\n    fn as_ref(&self) -> &str {\n        &self.hash\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(feature = \"ja4\")]\nimpl JA4 {\n    #[inline]\n    fn version_to_ja4code(val: Option<TlsVersion>) -> &'static str {\n        match val {\n            Some(TlsVersion::Tls13) => \"13\",\n            Some(TlsVersion::Tls12) => \"12\",\n            Some(TlsVersion::Tls11) => \"11\",\n            Some(TlsVersion::Tls10) => \"10\",\n            Some(TlsVersion::Ssl30) => \"s3\",\n            // the TLS parser does not support SSL 1.0 and 2.0 hence no\n            // support for \"s1\"/\"s2\"\n            _ => \"00\",\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn format_alpn(alpn: Option<&Vec<u8>>) -> [char; 2] {\n        let mut ret = ['0', '0'];\n\n        if let Some(alpn) = alpn {\n            if !alpn.is_empty() {\n                // If the first ALPN value is only a single character, then that character is treated as both the first and last character.\n                if alpn.len() == 2 {\n                    // GREASE values are 2 bytes, so this could be one -- check\n                    let v: u16 = ((alpn[0] as u16) << 8) | alpn[alpn.len() - 1] as u16;\n                    if HandshakeParams::is_grease(v) {\n                        return ret;\n                    }\n                }\n                if !alpn[0].is_ascii_alphanumeric() || !alpn[alpn.len() - 1].is_ascii_alphanumeric()\n                {\n                    // If the first or last byte of the first ALPN is non-alphanumeric (meaning not 0x30-0x39, 0x41-0x5A, or 0x61-0x7A), then we print the first and last characters of the hex representation of the first ALPN instead.\n                    ret[0] = char::from(HEX[(alpn[0] >> 4) as usize]);\n                    ret[1] = char::from(HEX[(alpn[alpn.len() - 1] & 0xF) as usize]);\n                } else {\n                    ret[0] = char::from(alpn[0]);\n                    ret[1] = char::from(alpn[alpn.len() - 1]);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        ret\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(feature = \"ja4\")]\nimpl JA4Impl for JA4 {\n    fn try_new(hs: &HandshakeParams) -> Option<Self> {\n        // All non-GREASE extensions are stored to produce a more verbose, complete output\n        // of extensions but we need to omit ALPN & SNI extensions from the JA4_a hash.\n        let mut exts = hs\n            .extensions\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|&ext| {\n                *ext != TlsExtensionType::ApplicationLayerProtocolNegotiation\n                    && *ext != TlsExtensionType::ServerName\n            })\n            .collect::<Vec<&TlsExtensionType>>();\n\n        let alpn = Self::format_alpn(hs.alpns.first());\n\n        // Calculate JA4_a\n        let ja4_a = format!(\n            \"{proto}{version}{sni}{nof_c:02}{nof_e:02}{al1}{al2}\",\n            proto = if hs.quic { \"q\" } else { \"t\" },\n            version = Self::version_to_ja4code(hs.tls_version),\n            sni = if hs.domain { \"d\" } else { \"i\" },\n            nof_c = min(99, hs.ciphersuites.len()),\n            nof_e = min(99, hs.extensions.len()),\n            al1 = alpn[0],\n            al2 = alpn[1]\n        );\n\n        // Calculate JA4_b\n        let mut sorted_ciphers = hs.ciphersuites.to_vec();\n        sorted_ciphers.sort_by(|a, b| u16::from(*a).cmp(&u16::from(*b)));\n        let sorted_cipherstrings: Vec<String> = sorted_ciphers\n            .iter()\n            .map(|v| format!(\"{:04x}\", u16::from(*v)))\n            .collect();\n        let mut sha = Sha256::new();\n        let ja4_b_raw = sorted_cipherstrings.join(\",\");\n        sha.update(&ja4_b_raw);\n        let mut ja4_b = format!(\"{:x}\", sha.finalize_reset());\n        ja4_b.truncate(12);\n\n        // Calculate JA4_c\n        exts.sort_by(|&a, &b| u16::from(*a).cmp(&u16::from(*b)));\n        let sorted_extstrings: Vec<String> = exts\n            .into_iter()\n            .map(|&v| format!(\"{:04x}\", u16::from(v)))\n            .collect();\n        let ja4_c1_raw = sorted_extstrings.join(\",\");\n        let unsorted_sigalgostrings: Vec<String> = hs\n            .signature_algorithms\n            .iter()\n            .map(|v| format!(\"{:04x}\", (*v)))\n            .collect();\n        let ja4_c2_raw = unsorted_sigalgostrings.join(\",\");\n        let ja4_c_raw = format!(\"{}_{}\", ja4_c1_raw, ja4_c2_raw);\n        sha.update(&ja4_c_raw);\n        let mut ja4_c = format!(\"{:x}\", sha.finalize());\n        ja4_c.truncate(12);\n\n        Some(Self {\n            hash: format!(\"{}_{}_{}\", ja4_a, ja4_b, ja4_c),\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(not(feature = \"ja4\"))]\nimpl JA4Impl for JA4 {\n    fn try_new(_hs: &HandshakeParams) -> Option<Self> {\n        None\n    }\n}\n\n// C ABI\n#[cfg(feature = \"ja4\")]\n#[no_mangle]\npub unsafe extern \"C\" fn SCJA4GetHash(hs: &HandshakeParams, out: &mut [u8; JA4_HEX_LEN]) {\n    if let Some(ja4) = JA4::try_new(hs) {\n        out[0..JA4_HEX_LEN].copy_from_slice(ja4.as_ref().as_bytes());\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\n#[cfg(feature = \"ja4\")]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use tls_parser::{TlsCipherSuiteID, TlsExtensionType, TlsVersion};\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_format_alpn_ascii() {\n        let res = JA4::format_alpn(Some(&\"http/1.1\".as_bytes().to_vec()));\n        assert_eq!(res, ['h', '1']);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_add_alpn_non_ascii_first_or_last() {\n        let res = JA4::format_alpn(Some(&vec![0x01, b'T', b'E', 0x7f])); // non-alphanumeric start and end\n        assert_eq!(res,
